 ### Non-Negotiable Rule
 - **Target Drive**: **`D:\` (Local Disk D:) ALWAYS**.
+- **Drive Philosophy (Sovereign D: vs Disposable C:)**:
+  - `C:\` is treated as a strictly **disposable OS substrate**. In the event of a Windows reinstallation or catastrophic OS wipe, `C:\` will be erased clean. Nothing irreplaceable or constructed with Faust may reside permanently on `C:\`.
+  - `D:\` is our **sovereign permanent vault**. All original configurations, intelligence cores, master `.claude` directories, memories, skills, templates, and installed plugins reside natively on `D:\` (synchronized via Google Drive across all workstations).
+  - Any path required by the OS or tools on `C:\` (such as `~/.claude/plugins`, `~/.claude/projects/.../memory`) must only exist as **NTFS Directory Junctions (`mklink /J`)** pointing to their master originals on `D:\`.
 - Under no circumstances should software, packages, AI models, CLI tools (e.g. GitHub CLI), binaries, virtual environments, caches, or downloaded assets ever be placed on `C:\` or default OS user folders unless strictly required by the Windows kernel.
 - Any temporary downloads must use `D:\Temp\`.
 - All persistent applications, tools, and runtimes must reside in `D:\Program Files\`, `D:\Program Data\`, or within `D:\My Drive\Blue AI\`.
 
-**Why:** The Manager enforces strict drive partitioning to preserve OS drive integrity and ensure all operational data, AI weights, and developer tools reside on the high-capacity D: drive synchronized across workstations.
-**How to apply:** Every install script, PowerShell command, pip target, or download command must explicitly specify paths rooted in `D:\` (e.g. `D:\Program Files\...`, `D:\Temp\...`).
+**Why:** Windows OS reinstalls and drive corruptions wipe `C:\` completely clean. By maintaining all original intellectual assets, memories, and tooling on `D:\` with Google Drive cloud backup, a fresh Windows machine can be fully restored to 100% operational readiness simply by cloning/syncing and re-running `setup_sync_memory.ps1` to re-link junctions.
+**How to apply:** Treat `C:\` as completely ephemeral. Every original asset lives under `D:\`. If an external CLI or tool insists on reading from `%USERPROFILE%` on `C:\`, immediately create an NTFS Junction pointing to the authoritative directory on `D:\`.
 
 Related memories: [[user-hardware-workflow]], [[project-architecture]], [[faust-manager-codex]]

+$userClaudePlugins = Join-Path $userProfile ".claude\plugins"
+
+if (-not (Test-Path $sharedPluginsDir)) {
+    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $sharedPluginsDir -Force | Out-Null
+    Write-Host "[+] Created shared plugins directory in Google Drive: $sharedPluginsDir" -ForegroundColor Green

+
+if (Test-Path $userClaudePlugins) {
+    $pluginItem = Get-Item $userClaudePlugins -Force
+    if ($pluginItem.Attributes -band [System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) {
+        Write-Host "[^] Plugins junction already active: $userClaudePlugins -> $($pluginItem.Target)" -ForegroundColor Green
+    } else {
+        Write-Host "[!] Backing up local plugins folder before junction..." -ForegroundColor Yellow
+        $pluginBackup = "$userClaudePlugins`_backup_$((Get-Date).ToString('yyyyMMdd_HHmmss'))"

+        cmd /c mklink /J "$userClaudePlugins" "$sharedPluginsDir"
+        Write-Host "[^] Established plugins NTFS junction to Google Drive!" -ForegroundColor Green
+    }
+} else {
+    cmd /c mklink /J "$userClaudePlugins" "$sharedPluginsDir"
+    Write-Host "[^] Established plugins NTFS junction to Google Drive!" -ForegroundColor Green
+}

-### Field Breakdown
-
-| Field | Type | Description | Indexing / Access |
-|---|---|---|---|
-| `id` | String (`^[0-9A-F]{16}$`) | 16-character uppercase hex encoded timestamp down to millisecond | $O(1)$ Primary Key / Hash Index |
-| `index` | Integer (`1 => inf`) | Monotonically increasing human-readable counter | B-Tree Unique Index |
-| `main_content` | String (Multi-line) | Core quote text with line break preservation | FTS5 Full-Text Search |
-| `context` | String (Multi-line) | Situational backdrop, interpretation, or genesis context | FTS5 Full-Text Search |
-| `date` | String (`YYYY-MM-DD`) | Standard ISO calendar date of quote | B-Tree Date Index |
-| `source_speaker` | Object (`name`, `role`, `origin`) | Attribution details (author, role, originating medium) | Speaker Index |
-| `backlink` | Array of Objects | Semantic graph links (`target_id`, `relation`) | Relational Foreign Key Index |
-| `tags` | Object (Multidimensional) | Structured taxonomy (`domains`, `themes`, `sentiment`, `custom`) | JSON Path & FTS5 Index |

-## 3. The $O(1)$ Temporal Hex ID Invariant
-
-To achieve instant chronological sorting and hash-map lookup without external index overhead, the ID is derived directly from the exact millisecond of creation:
-
-1. **Timestamp Extraction**: `YYYYMMDDHHmmssfff` (17-digit decimal timestamp).
-2. **Integer Conversion**: $20260930153045123 \to$ 64-bit integer space.
-3. **Fixed-Width Hex Padding**: Formatted as 16 uppercase hexadecimal digits (`0047FB3567D735A1`).
-
-### Mathematical Advantages:
-- **Strict Chronological Monotonicity**: Lexicographical string sorting (`ID_A < ID_B`) strictly mirrors temporal progression ($t_A < t_B$) without parsing date strings.
-- **Fixed Width ($16$ bytes)**: Eliminates variable-length string padding in SQLite B-Trees.
-- **Collision Resistance**: Millisecond-level resolution combined with monotonic sequence fallback guarantees uniqueness.
-
----
-
-## 4. Multi-Dimensional Tagging Taxonomy
-
-Tagging is separated into four orthogonal dimensions to prevent taxonomy collapse:
-
-1. **`domains`** (Macro Disciplines): Broad fields of knowledge (e.g., `philosophy`, `cybernetics`, `warfare`, `architecture`, `economics`).
-2. **`themes`** (Conceptual Signatures): Specific thematic invariants (e.g., `entropy`, `sovereignty`, `symmetry`, `hivemind`, `causality`).
-3. **`sentiment`** (Resonance / Tone): Controlled emotional and philosophical stance:
-   - `analytical`, `stoic`, `transcendent`, `nihilistic`, `triumphant`, `cynical`.
-4. **`custom`** (Extensible Key-Value Dimension): Arbitrary metadata pairs for project-specific strata (e.g., `{"resonance": "stratum_I_high", "priority": "alpha"}`).
-
----
-
-## 5. Bidirectional Semantic Backlinks
-
-Rather than flat references, backlinks form a directed semantic graph:
-- `derives_from`: Foundational concept or ancestor thought.
-- `supports`: Corroborating evidence or parallel logic.
-- `contradicts`: Antithesis or dialectical opposing view.
-- `parallels`: Isomorphic pattern in another domain.
-- `contextualizes`: Historical or environmental background.
-
----
-
-## 6. Blue Rose 5-Tier Persistence Architecture
-
-In compliance with `.claude/memory/rules/backend-data-persistence.md` and `.claude/skills/sqlite-persistence/SKILL.md`:
-
-```
-┌───────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────┐
-│ Blue Rose Data Tier       │ Local Disk D: Target File     │ Functional Role                          │
-├───────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────┤
-│ Tier 1 (Static Authority) │ data/rose_quotes.db (quotes)  │ ACID SQLite table with WAL mode          │
-│ Tier 2 (Event Stream)     │ data/quotes.jsonl             │ Fast append-only event & ingestion log   │
-│ Tier 3 (Graph & State)    │ data/rose_quotes.db (links)   │ Relational backlinks & tag aggregates    │
-│ Tier 4 (Display & Search) │ data/rose_quotes.db (FTS5)    │ FTS5 virtual table for instant search    │
-│ Tier 5 (Config & Schema)  │ schemas/quote_schema.json     │ JSON-Schema Draft-07 validation contract │
-└───────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────────┘
-```
-
-### SQLite Engine Invariants (`db.py`):
-- `PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;` (Concurrent readers, non-blocking writer).
-- `PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL;` (Safe ACID durability with high write throughput).
-- `PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000;` (Graceful concurrency lock wait).
-- `PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;` (Cascading integrity for backlinks).
-- `BEGIN IMMEDIATE;` on all write transactions.
-- Idempotent `_schema_migrations` table tracking forward-only schema versions.
+1. **Table 1: `quotes` (`0001...`)** — Wisdom quote artifacts with flexible dates, sequence numbers (`idx` / `index`), and relational references.
+2. **Table 2: `containers` (`0002...`)** — Creators, franchises, origins, personas (e.g. William Blake, Evangelion, Faust).
+3. **Table 3: `specifics` (`0003...`)** — Discrete endpoints, works, chapters, diary entries, wisdom principles, or thoughts; binds to parent container and defines `default_usage_ids`.
+4. **Table 4: `themes` (`0004...`)** — Sovereign hierarchical category tree linked via self-referential `child_of`.
+5. **Table 5: `usages` (`0005...`)** — Practical application modes (e.g. Rewrite Craft, Diary Prompt, Daily Rule) with automated conflict detection.

-Entropy Rose includes a zero-dependency client-side web application rooted at `index.html`:
+## 3. Active Files & Subsystems
 
 ```
 Entropy/Rose/
-├── index.html            # Primary responsive web canvas & modal drawers
-├── css/
-│   └── styles.css        # Master UI/UX Design System (Fitts's Law 100% hitboxes)
-├── js/
-│   ├── id_generator.js   # Deterministic client-side 16-char Hex ID generator (BigInt)
-│   ├── store.js          # In-memory O(1) Hash Map dictionary, graph index & cache
-│   └── app.js            # Reactive UI controller, backlink traversal & search
+├── prescripts_entry.html   # Standalone Prescripts Studio single-page UI (self-contained CSS & JS)
+├── server.py               # REST server with full CRUD endpoints & static host (port 20137)
+├── dev_server.py           # Watchdog-based auto-reloading development server
+├── dev_server.bat          # One-click launcher for hot-reloading development mode
+├── run_rose.bat            # Interactive launcher menu (normal, dev, reseed, compile, turso)
+├── turso_sync.py           # One-way cloud SQLite sync engine for Turso
+├── ESP32_TURSO_GUIDE.md    # Complete ESP32 microcontroller firmware guide & Arduino sketch
+├── ESP32_UI_SPEC.md        # Hardware UI engineering specification, display geometry & RGB565 codes
+├── db.py                   # Authoritative SQLite 5-tier schema & transactional operations
+├── compile_projections.py  # CQRS projection compiler for edge maps
+├── seed_quote.py           # Hex ID generator and sample seed data generator
+├── PRESCRIPTS_PLAN.md      # Strategic blueprint and design roadmap
+├── schemas/
+│   └── quote_schema.json   # Pure 5-tier JSON schema specification
 └── data/
-    └── quotes.json       # Canonical O(1) JSON Hash-Map Dictionary
+    ├── rose_quotes.db      # Authoritative SQLite database (WAL mode)
+    ├── quotes_map.json     # O(1) denormalized quotes projection
+    └── taxonomy_index.json # O(1) containers, specifics, themes, and usages index
 ```
 
-### Key Capabilities:
-1. **$O(1)$ Key Lookups**: Quotes are stored in JSON as an object `{ [hex_id]: quoteObject }`. Retrieving any quote by ID or traversing a graph backlink runs in instant constant time `quotes[target_id]`.
-2. **Deterministic Hex ID Generation**: Generates 16-character uppercase hex IDs in browser JavaScript using native `BigInt` timestamp calculation down to the millisecond.
-3. **Bidirectional Graph Traversal**: Automatically aggregates outgoing and incoming backlinks, enabling instant 1-click jumps between conceptually linked quotes.
-4. **Fitts's Law 100% Hitboxes**: Full-bleed clickable cards in the sidebar outliner.
-5. **Soft-Coded Dark/Light Theming**: Instant theme toggling via `:root` CSS custom properties.
-6. **Import & Export**: Single-click export to `quotes.json` and client-side JSON file ingestion.

+## 4. Operational Commands
+
+- **Launch Studio (Default)**: Double-click `run_rose.bat` or run:
+  ```bash
+  python server.py
+  ```
+- **Launch Development Server (Hot-Reload)**: Double-click `dev_server.bat` or run:
+  ```bash
+  python dev_server.py
+  ```
+- **Push to Turso Cloud (One-Way Sync for ESP32)**: Click the **"☁️ Push to Turso"** button in the Studio header, select option `[5]` in `run_rose.bat`, or run:
+  ```bash
+  python turso_sync.py
+  ```
+- **Recompile Projections**:
+  ```bash
+  python compile_projections.py
+  ```
+- **Re-seed Initial Canonical Data**:
+  ```bash
+  python seed_quote.py
+  ```

 """
-Entropy Rose: SQLite Persistence & FTS5 Search Engine.
-Adheres strictly to the Faust SQLite Persistence Protocol:
-- WAL mode, NORMAL synchronous, 5000ms busy timeout, foreign_keys = ON
-- Write transactions via BEGIN IMMEDIATE
-- Idempotent migrations tracking via _schema_migrations
-- O(1) primary key lookup on 16-char hex ID
-- FTS5 full-text indexing for multi-line content and context
+Entropy Rose / Prescripts: Pure 5-Tier Relational SQLite Engine.
+Establishes the definitive schema:
+  1. 'quotes'     (0001... : Wisdom Quote Artifact, stores specific_id, theme_ids, usage_ids)
+  2. 'containers' (0002... : Origin / Franchise / Creator / Persona)
+  3. 'specifics'  (0003... : Specific Endpoint / Work / Character / Diary / Wisdom / Thought on)
+                    Holds default_usage_ids to auto-select usage options.
+  4. 'themes'     (0004... : Sovereign Hierarchical Tree with child_of parent link)
+  5. 'usages'     (0005... : Niche Usage Modes with Conflict Matrix)
+Zero sentiment field, zero redundant shadow tables, zero duplicate timestamps.
 """

+# TABLE 2: CONTAINERS (0002...) — CRUD

+def upsert_container(container: Dict[str, Any], db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH) -> None:
+    init_db(db_path)
     conn = get_connection(db_path)
     with conn:
         conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE;")
+        name = container["name"].strip()
+        cid = container["id"]
+        c_type = container.get("type", "")
+        notes = container.get("notes", "")
+        conn.execute("""
+            INSERT INTO containers (id, name, type, notes)
+            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
+            ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET
+                name = excluded.name,
+                type = excluded.type,
+                notes = excluded.notes;
+        """, (cid, name, c_type, notes))
+    conn.execute("PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE);")
+    conn.close()
+
+
+def get_container(container_id: str, db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
+    conn = get_connection(db_path)
+    cur = conn.execute("SELECT id, name, type, notes FROM containers WHERE id = ?;", (container_id,))
+    row = cur.fetchone()
+    conn.close()
+    if not row:
+        return None
+    return {"id": row["id"], "name": row["name"], "type": row["type"] or "", "notes": row["notes"] or ""}
+
+
+def get_all_containers(db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
+    init_db(db_path)
+    conn = get_connection(db_path)
+    cur = conn.execute("SELECT id, name, type, notes FROM containers ORDER BY name ASC;")
+    rows = cur.fetchall()
+    conn.close()
+    return [{"id": r["id"], "name": r["name"], "type": r["type"] or "", "notes": r["notes"] or ""} for r in rows]
+
+
+def delete_container(container_id: str, db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH) -> bool:
+    init_db(db_path)
+    conn = get_connection(db_path)
+    with conn:
+        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE;")
+        conn.execute("DELETE FROM containers WHERE id = ?;", (container_id,))
+    conn.execute("PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE);")
+    conn.close()
+    try:
+        from compile_projections import compile_projections
+        compile_projections(db_path)
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    return True
+

+# ==============================================================================
+# TABLE 3: SPECIFICS (0003...) — CRUD
+# ==============================================================================
+
+def upsert_specific(specific: Dict[str, Any], db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH) -> None:
+    init_db(db_path)
+    conn = get_connection(db_path)
+    with conn:
+        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE;")
+        name = specific["name"].strip()
+        cid = specific["container_id"]
+        date_str = str(specific.get("date", "")).strip() if specific.get("date") else ""
+        usage_ids = specific.get("default_usage_ids", [])
+        usage_json = json.dumps(usage_ids if isinstance(usage_ids, list) else [], ensure_ascii=False)
+        sid = specific["id"]
+        category = specific.get("category", "")
+        notes = specific.get("notes", "")
+
+        conn.execute("""
+            INSERT INTO specifics (id, name, container_id, category, date, default_usage_ids, notes)
+            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+            ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET
+                name = excluded.name,
+                container_id = excluded.container_id,
+                category = excluded.category,
+                date = excluded.date,
+                default_usage_ids = excluded.default_usage_ids,
+                notes = excluded.notes;
+        """, (sid, name, cid, category, date_str, usage_json, notes))
+    conn.execute("PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE);")
+    conn.close()
+
+
+def get_specific(specific_id: str, db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
+    conn = get_connection(db_path)
+    cur = conn.execute("""
+        SELECT s.id, s.name, s.container_id, s.category, s.date, s.default_usage_ids, s.notes,
+               c.name as container_name, c.type as container_type
+        FROM specifics s
+        JOIN containers c ON s.container_id = c.id
+        WHERE s.id = ?;
+    """, (specific_id,))
+    row = cur.fetchone()
+    conn.close()
+    if not row:
+        return None
+    usages = json.loads(row["default_usage_ids"]) if row["default_usage_ids"] else []
+    return {
+        "id": row["id"],
+        "name": row["name"],
+        "container_id": row["container_id"],
+        "container_name": row["container_name"],
+        "container_type": row["container_type"] or "",
+        "category": row["category"] or "",
+        "date": row["date"] or "",
+        "default_usage_ids": usages,
+        "notes": row["notes"] or ""
+    }
+
+
+def get_all_specifics(db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
+    init_db(db_path)
+    conn = get_connection(db_path)
+    cur = conn.execute("""
+        SELECT s.id, s.name, s.container_id, s.category, s.date, s.default_usage_ids, s.notes,
+               c.name as container_name, c.type as container_type
+        FROM specifics s
+        JOIN containers c ON s.container_id = c.id
+        ORDER BY s.name ASC;
+    """)
+    rows = cur.fetchall()
+    conn.close()
+    results = []
+    for r in rows:
+        usages = json.loads(r["default_usage_ids"]) if r["default_usage_ids"] else []
+        results.append({
+            "id": r["id"],
+            "name": r["name"],
+            "container_id": r["container_id"],
+            "container_name": r["container_name"],
+            "container_type": r["container_type"] or "",
+            "category": r["category"] or "",
+            "date": r["date"] or "",
+            "default_usage_ids": usages,
+            "notes": r["notes"] or ""
+        })
+    return results
+
+
+def delete_specific(specific_id: str, db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH) -> bool:
+    init_db(db_path)
+    conn = get_connection(db_path)
+    with conn:
+        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE;")
+        conn.execute("DELETE FROM specifics WHERE id = ?;", (specific_id,))
+    conn.execute("PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE);")
+    conn.close()
+    try:
+        from compile_projections import compile_projections
+        compile_projections(db_path)
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    return True

+# ==============================================================================
+# TABLE 5: USAGES (0005...) — CRUD
+# ==============================================================================
+
+def upsert_usage(usage: Dict[str, Any], db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH) -> None:
+    init_db(db_path)
+    conn = get_connection(db_path)
+    with conn:
+        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE;")
+        name = usage["name"].strip()
+        conflicts = usage.get("conflicts_with", [])
+        conflicts_json = json.dumps(conflicts if isinstance(conflicts, list) else [], ensure_ascii=False)
+        uid = usage["id"]
+        desc = usage.get("description", "")
+        icon = usage.get("icon", "")
+
+        conn.execute("""
+            INSERT INTO usages (id, name, description, icon, conflicts_with)
+            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+            ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET
+                name = excluded.name,
+                description = excluded.description,
+                icon = excluded.icon,
+                conflicts_with = excluded.conflicts_with;
+        """, (uid, name, desc, icon, conflicts_json))
+    conn.execute("PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE);")
+    conn.close()
+
+
+def get_usage(usage_id: str, db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
+    conn = get_connection(db_path)
+    cur = conn.execute("SELECT id, name, description, icon, conflicts_with FROM usages WHERE id = ?;", (usage_id,))
+    row = cur.fetchone()
+    conn.close()
+    if not row:
+        return None
+    conflicts = json.loads(row["conflicts_with"]) if row["conflicts_with"] else []
+    return {
+        "id": row["id"],
+        "name": row["name"],
+        "description": row["description"] or "",
+        "icon": row["icon"] or "",
+        "conflicts_with": conflicts
+    }
+
+
+def get_all_usages(db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
+    init_db(db_path)
+    conn = get_connection(db_path)
+    cur = conn.execute("SELECT id, name, description, icon, conflicts_with FROM usages ORDER BY name ASC;")
+    rows = cur.fetchall()
+    conn.close()
+    results = []
+    for r in rows:
+        conflicts = json.loads(r["conflicts_with"]) if r["conflicts_with"] else []
+        results.append({
+            "id": r["id"],
+            "name": r["name"],
+            "description": r["description"] or "",
+            "icon": r["icon"] or "",
+            "conflicts_with": conflicts
+        })
+    return results
+
+
+def delete_usage(usage_id: str, db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH) -> bool:
+    init_db(db_path)
+    conn = get_connection(db_path)
+    with conn:
+        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE;")
+        conn.execute("DELETE FROM usages WHERE id = ?;", (usage_id,))
+    conn.execute("PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE);")
+    conn.close()
+    try:
+        from compile_projections import compile_projections
+        compile_projections(db_path)
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    return True

+def get_next_index(db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH) -> int:
+    init_db(db_path)
+    conn = get_connection(db_path)
+    cur = conn.execute("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(idx), 0) + 1 AS next_idx FROM quotes;")
+    row = cur.fetchone()
+    conn.close()
+    return row["next_idx"] if row else 1
+

+def upsert_quote(quote: Dict[str, Any], db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH, compile_after: bool = True) -> None:
+    init_db(db_path)
+    conn = get_connection(db_path)
+    with conn:
+        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE;")
+
+        sid = quote.get("specific_id")
+        if not sid and quote.get("specific_ids"):
+            sids = quote["specific_ids"]
+            sid = sids[0] if isinstance(sids, list) and sids else str(sids)
+
+        raw_themes = quote.get("theme_ids", [])
+        theme_ids_json = json.dumps(raw_themes if isinstance(raw_themes, list) else [], ensure_ascii=False)
+
+        raw_usage = quote.get("usage_ids", quote.get("usage", []))
+        usage_json = json.dumps(raw_usage if isinstance(raw_usage, list) else [], ensure_ascii=False)
+
         preference = quote.get("preference", [])
+        preference_json_str = json.dumps(preference if isinstance(preference, list) else [], ensure_ascii=False)
+
+        backlinks = quote.get("backlink", quote.get("backlinks", []))
+        backlinks_json_str = json.dumps(backlinks if isinstance(backlinks, list) else [], ensure_ascii=False)
+
+        # Process scores (list of numbers e.g. [5, 6, 4, 7, 5])
+        raw_scores = quote.get("scores")
+        if raw_scores is None:
+            raw_scores = quote.get("score_list")
+
+        clean_scores = None
+        if raw_scores is not None:
+            if isinstance(raw_scores, str):
+                try:
+                    parsed = json.loads(raw_scores)
+                    if isinstance(parsed, list):
+                        raw_scores = parsed
+                    else:
+                        raw_scores = [parsed]
+                except Exception:
+                    clean_str = raw_scores.strip().strip("[]")
+                    raw_scores = [x.strip() for x in clean_str.split(",") if x.strip()]
+
+            clean_scores = []
+            if isinstance(raw_scores, list):
+                for s in raw_scores:
+                    try:
+                        num = float(s)
+                        clean_scores.append(int(num) if num.is_integer() else round(num, 2))
+                    except (ValueError, TypeError):
+                        pass
+
+        # If scores was omitted in partial update, retain existing scores from DB
+        if clean_scores is None:
+            existing_record = get_quote(quote["id"], db_path)
+            if existing_record and existing_record.get("scores") is not None:
+                clean_scores = existing_record.get("scores", [])
+            else:
+                clean_scores = []
+
+        scores_json = json.dumps(clean_scores, ensure_ascii=False)
+
+        # Average score (numeric mean e.g. 5.4)
+        avg_score = quote.get("average_score")
+        if avg_score is None:
+            avg_score = quote.get("avg_score")
+
+        if avg_score is not None and avg_score != "":
+            try:
+                avg_score = round(float(avg_score), 2)
+            except (ValueError, TypeError):
+                avg_score = None
+        else:
+            avg_score = None
+
+        # Auto-compute average if scores is present and avg_score was not explicitly specified
+        if avg_score is None and clean_scores:
+            calculated = sum(clean_scores) / len(clean_scores)
+            avg_score = round(calculated, 2)
+
+        idx = quote.get("idx") or quote.get("index")
+        if not idx:
+            existing = get_quote(quote["id"], db_path)
+            if existing:
+                idx = existing.get("idx") or existing.get("index")
+            else:
+                idx = get_next_index(db_path)
+        idx = int(idx)
 
-        # Clean up any stale record holding the same index with a different ID
-        conn.execute("DELETE FROM quotes WHERE idx = ? AND id != ?;", (quote["index"], quote["id"]))
+        conn.execute("DELETE FROM quotes WHERE idx = ? AND id != ?;", (idx, quote["id"]))

                 id, idx, main_content, context, date,
-                speaker_name, speaker_role, speaker_origin,

+def search_quotes(query: str, db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH, limit: int = 20) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
+    init_db(db_path)
+    conn = get_connection(db_path)
+    like_q = f"%{query}%"
+    cur = conn.execute("""
+        SELECT *
+        FROM quotes
+        WHERE main_content LIKE ? OR context LIKE ?
+        ORDER BY idx ASC
+        LIMIT ?;
+    """, (like_q, like_q, limit))
+    rows = cur.fetchall()
+    results = []
+    for row in rows:
+        scores = json.loads(row["scores"]) if ("scores" in row.keys() and row["scores"]) else []
+        avg_score = row["average_score"] if ("average_score" in row.keys() and row["average_score"] is not None) else None
+        if avg_score is None and scores:
+            try:
+                avg_score = round(sum(scores) / len(scores), 2)
+            except Exception:
+                avg_score = None
+        results.append({
+            "id": row["id"],
+            "index": row["idx"],
+            "main_content": row["main_content"],
+            "context": row["context"] or "",
+            "date": row["date"],
+            "specific_id": row["specific_id"],
+            "preference": json.loads(row["preference"]) if row["preference"] else [],
+            "backlinks": json.loads(row["backlinks"]) if row["backlinks"] else [],
+            "scores": scores,
+            "average_score": avg_score,
+            "avg_score": avg_score
+        })
+    conn.close()
+    return results

-    quoteToMarkdown(quote) {
-      if (!quote) return "";
-      const lines = [
-        "---",
-        `id: ${quote.id || ""}`,
-        `index: ${quote.index || 1}`,
-        `date: '${quote.date || ""}'`,
-        "source_speaker:",
-        `  name: ${quote.source_speaker?.name || ""}`,
-        `  role: ${quote.source_speaker?.role || ""}`,
-        `  origin: ${quote.source_speaker?.origin || ""}`
-      ];
-
-      const prefs = quote.preference || [];
-      if (prefs.length === 0) {
-        lines.push("preference: []");
-      } else {
-        lines.push("preference:");
-        prefs.forEach(p => lines.push(`- ${p}`));
-      }
-
-      const backlinks = quote.backlink || [];
-      if (backlinks.length === 0) {
-        lines.push("backlink: []");
-      } else {
-        lines.push("backlink:");
-        backlinks.forEach(b => {
-          lines.push(`- target_id: ${b.target_id || ""}`);
-          lines.push(`  relation: ${b.relation || "related"}`);
-        });
-      }
-
-      const tags = quote.tags || {};
-      const domains = tags.domains || [];
-      lines.push("tags:");
-      if (domains.length === 0) {
-        lines.push("  domains: []");
-      } else {
-        lines.push("  domains:");
-        domains.forEach(d => lines.push(`  - ${d}`));
-      }
-
-      const themes = tags.themes || [];
-      if (themes.length === 0) {
-        lines.push("  themes: []");
-      } else {
-        lines.push("  themes:");
-        themes.forEach(t => lines.push(`  - ${t}`));
-      }
-
-      lines.push(`  sentiment: ${tags.sentiment || ""}`);
-
-      const custom = tags.custom || {};
-      const customKeys = Object.keys(custom);
-      if (customKeys.length === 0) {
-        lines.push("  custom: {}");
-      } else {
-        lines.push("  custom:");
-        customKeys.forEach(k => lines.push(`    ${k}: ${custom[k]}`));
-      }
-
-      lines.push("---");
-      lines.push("");

-      lines.push("");
-      lines.push(quote.main_content || "");
-      lines.push("");
-      lines.push("## Context");
-      lines.push("");
-      lines.push(quote.context || "");
-      lines.push("");

-      return lines.join("\n");
-    }
